<h2 class="h4 grid-view-item__title"><a href="/collections/best-seller/products/remmy-retro-round-sunglasses" title="Remmy">Remmy</a></h2>
<div class="grid-view-item__types">
   Slate Crystal/Bottle Green
   </div>
<div class="colect-descr"><p>The Remmy is our calling card and they will never let you down. We took a note from what we know works, inspired by retro round sunglasses that captured our eye, the Remmy carries the look with a modern flare. Available in two sizes: the original 52 is a touch larger and conquers the perfectly oversized look for women, while the 49 is a slightly refined fit suited best for males. Their ultra-light construction features a slight horned temple and notched bridge.</p></div>

Taking the best of the Squire and bringing it into present day, Friar is a staple rectangular, masculine frame. Incorporating beveled detailing throughout, while maintaining a light presence, Friar makes for an easy all day wear.</div>

  function getCartItem() {
    $('.usertools__group--cart').empty();
	var html = '';
    $.getJSON('/cart.js', function(cart) {
      var item_s = '';
      if(cart.item_count > 1){
        item_s = 'items';
      } else {
        item_s = 'item';        
      }        
      html += '<a href="/cart" class="dropdown__title usertools__link usertools__title"><span class="icon--bag1"><img src="//cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/0008/6156/9085/t/20/assets/cart-bag.svg?8414218600835007417"></span><span class="icon__fallback-text">My Cart</span>';
      html += '<div id="CartCount" class="site-header__cart-count"><span>' + cart.item_count +'</span><span class="icon__fallback-text medium-up--hide">'+item_s+'</span></div>';
      html += '</a>';
      if(cart.item_count > 0){
        var items = cart.items;
       	html += '<div class="usertools__dropdown usertools__dropdown--cart">';
        $(items).each(function(index) {
          var item = items[index];
          html += '<div class="minicart" data-quantity="' + item.quantity + '">';
          html += '<ul class="minicart__list">';
          html += '<li class="minicart__item cart-item">';          
          html += '<a href="'+item.url+'" class="minicart__link">';
          html += '<img alt="'+item.title+'" src="'+item.image+'" class="minicart__image"></a>';
          html += '<div class="minicart-details">';
          html += '<h4 class="minicart__name"><a href="'+item.url+'">'+item.product_title+'<span>( '+item.quantity+' )</span></a></h4>';
          html += '<p class="minicart__price price"><span class="money">'+ theme.Currency.formatMoney(item.price, theme.moneyFormat) +'</span></p>';
          html += '<a class="minicart__remove remove" data-id="'+ item.id +'" href="javascript:void(0);">Remove</a></div></li></ul>';
          html += '</div>';
        });        
        html += '<div class="minicart-total">';
        html += '<p class="minicart-total__info">Subtotal<span class="minicart-total__amount amount">'+ theme.Currency.formatMoney(cart.total_price, theme.moneyFormat) +'</span></p>';
        html += '<a href="/cart/" class="btn minicart-total__link">Go To cart</a>';
        html += '</div></div>';
        
      } else {
        html += '<div class="usertools__dropdown usertools__dropdown--cart">';
        html += '<div class="minicart" data-quantity="0">';                                    
        html += '<h4 class="minicart__title">Nothing in your cart yet.</h4>';
        html += '</div></div>';
      }
      
      $('.usertools__group--cart').empty();
      $('.usertools__group--cart').append(html);  
              
      $('.minicart__remove.remove').click(function(){
        var id = $(this).attr('data-id');
        updateCartItem(id,0,$(this));
      })
      
    });
  }  
  
  function updateCartItem(cid,qty,element) {    
    $.ajax({
      type: "POST",
      url: '/cart/change.js',
      data: 'quantity='+qty+'&id='+cid,
      success: function(result) {
        getCartItem(); 
      },
      error: function(data, errorThrown)
      { 
        var cart = JSON.parse(data.responseText);
        $('.site-header__cart-count#CartCount').html(cart.item_count);
        $('.usertools__dropdown--cart .minicart-total .minicart-total__info .minicart-total__amount').html(theme.Currency.formatMoney(cart.total_price, theme.moneyFormat));
        if(element){
          element.parent().closest('.minicart').remove();
        }

        if(cart.item_count == 0){
          var html = "";
          html += '<a href="/cart" class="dropdown__title usertools__link usertools__title"><span class="icon--bag1"><img src="//cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/0008/6156/9085/t/20/assets/cart-bag.svg?8414218600835007417"></span><span class="icon__fallback-text">My Cart</span>';
          html += '<div id="CartCount" class="site-header__cart-count"><span>' + cart.item_count +'</span><span class="icon__fallback-text medium-up--hide">item</span></div>';
          html += '</a>';
          html += '<div class="usertools__dropdown usertools__dropdown--cart">';
          html += '<div class="minicart" data-quantity="0">';                                    
          html += '<h4 class="minicart__title">Nothing in your cart yet.</h4>';
          html += '</div></div>';
          
          $('site-header__cart-count#CartCount').text(0);
          $('.usertools__group--cart').html(html);          
          
        }
        // getCartItem();
      }
    });         
  }
  
  function showMinicart() {
    $('html, body').animate({ scrollTop: $('.header-section').offset().top }, 800);
    $('.usertools__group--cart').addClass('showMincart');
    
    setTimeout(function(){
    	$('.usertools__group--cart').removeClass('showMincart');
    },4000);    
  }
  
  
  function addToCart(variantId,qty) {
      $.getJSON('/cart.js', function(cart) {
        var items = cart.items;
          if(cart.item_count > 0){
            if(items[0].id == 7943202832445 || items[0].id == 7943202766909 || items[0].id == 7943202799677 || items[0].id == 7943202865213)
            {
              $('.err_msg').remove();
              $('.product-form-item').prepend('<p class="err_msg">Gift cards must be purchased separately</p>');
              $('.usertools__dropdown--cart').prepend('<p class="err_msg">Gift cards must be purchased separately</p>');
              console.log('Gift cards must be purchased separately');
               console.log(cart)
               return false;
            }else{
              if(variantId == 7943202766909 || variantId == 7943202799677 || variantId == 7943202832445 || variantId == 7943202865213)
              {
                $('.err_msg').remove();
                 $('.product-form-item').prepend('<p class="err_msg">Gift cards must be purchased separately</p>');
                $('.usertools__dropdown--cart').prepend('<p class="err_msg">Gift cards must be purchased separately</p>');
                return false;
              }else{
                $.ajax({

      }
    });
              }  
            }

      },
      error: function(error){

      });
   
    showMinicart();
    
  }

(function () {
  if ( typeof window.CustomEvent === "function" ) return false; //If not IE

  function CustomEvent ( event, params ) {
    params = params || { bubbles: false, cancelable: false, detail: undefined };
    var evt = document.createEvent( 'CustomEvent' );
    evt.initCustomEvent( event, params.bubbles, params.cancelable, params.detail );
    return evt;
  }

  CustomEvent.prototype = window.Event.prototype;

  window.CustomEvent = CustomEvent;
})();

<script async="true" src="//static.criteo.net/js/ld/ld.js" type="text/javascript"></script>
<script type="text/javascript">
(function(){

function priceFromCents(cents) {
    if (typeof(cents) != "number")
        return;

    return (cents / 100);
}

var deviceType = /iPad/.test(navigator.userAgent) ? "t" : /Mobile|iP(hone|od)|Android|BlackBerry|IEMobile|Silk/.test(navigator.userAgent) ? "m" : "d";
window.criteo_q = window.criteo_q || [];
window.criteo_q.push(
  { event: "setAccount", account: 38830 },
  { event: "setEmail", email: "" },
  { event: "setSiteType", type: deviceType },
  { event: "viewHome" }
  
);

function isRelevantCartUpdate(settings) {
    function dataContains(term) {
        return settings.data && settings.data.indexOf && settings.data.indexOf(term) > -1;
    }

    return (settings.url == "/cart/add.js" || settings.url == "/cart/update.js")
      && (
        dataContains('attributes[prod') ||
        (dataContains('id=') && dataContains('quantity='))
      );
}

$(document).ajaxSuccess(function(event, xhr, settings) {
    if (isRelevantCartUpdate(settings)) {
        $.getJSON("/cart.js", function(data) {
            var basketData = [];
            var item;

            if (!data.items || !data.items.length)
                return;

            for (var i = 0; i < data.items.length; i++) {
                item = data.items[i];

                basketData.push({
                    id: item.product_id,
                    price: priceFromCents(item.price),
                    quantity: item.quantity
                });
            }

            window.criteo_q.push({
                event: "viewBasket",
                product: basketData
            });
        });
    }
});
})();
</script>
<!-- End Criteo OneTag -->
